\begin{abbrevlist}
\item $\varphi_{1} :\equiv \mathrm{ContOn}(f,[a,b]) \to \mathrm{ContOn}(f,[a,b])$
\item $\varphi_{2} :\equiv \forall x\,\forall y\,\forall z\,(x \leq y \to y \leq z \to x \leq z) \to \forall y\,\forall z\,(K \leq y \to y \leq z \to K \leq z)$
\item $\varphi_{3} :\equiv \neg (\mathrm{SubIdx}(s) \land c \in [a,b] \land \mathrm{Conv}(p \circ s,c))$
\item $\varphi_{4} :\equiv \forall x\,\forall y\,\forall z\,(x \leq y \to y \leq z \to x \leq z) \to \forall y\,\forall z\,(N_{1} \leq y \to y \leq z \to N_{1} \leq z)$
\item $\varphi_{5} :\equiv \exists c\,(\mathrm{SubIdx}(s) \land c \in [a,b] \land \mathrm{Conv}(p \circ s,c))$
\item $\varphi_{6} :\equiv \mathrm{ContOn}(f,[a,b]) \to a \in \mathbb{R} \to b \in \mathbb{R} \to \mathrm{RFn}(f)$
\item $\varphi_{7} :\equiv b \in \mathbb{R} \to \forall c \in [a,b]\,(\mathrm{ContIn}(f,[a,b],c))$
\item $\varphi_{8} :\equiv \mathrm{SubIdx}(s) \land c \in [a,b] \land \mathrm{Conv}(p \circ s,c) \to 0 < 0$
\item $\varphi_{9} :\equiv \forall x\,([a,b] \subseteq \mathrm{dom}(f) \to x \in [a,b] \to x \in \mathrm{dom}(f))$
\item $\varphi_{10} :\equiv \mathrm{SubIdx}(s) \land c \in [a,b] \land \mathrm{Conv}(p \circ s,c) \to \mathrm{NSeq}(s)$
\item $\varphi_{11} :\equiv \mathrm{Conv}(v_{848},v_{847}) \to \mathrm{Conv}(v_{845} \circ v_{848},v_{845}(v_{847}))$
\item $\varphi_{12} :\equiv \mathrm{Conv}(v_{842},v_{841}) \to \mathrm{Conv}(v_{839} \circ v_{842},v_{839}(v_{841}))$
\item $\varphi_{13} :\equiv \forall y\,\forall z\,(K \leq y \to y \leq z \to K \leq z) \to \forall z\,(K \leq N_{1} \cup K \to N_{1} \cup K \leq z \to K \leq z)$
\item $\varphi_{14} :\equiv \mathrm{SubIdx}(s) \land c \in [a,b] \land \mathrm{Conv}(p \circ s,c) \to \mathrm{SubIdx}(s)$
\item $\varphi_{15} :\equiv \mathrm{SubIdx}(s) \leftrightarrow \mathrm{NSeq}(s) \land \forall k \in \mathbb{N}\,(s_{k} < s_{S(k)})$
\item $\varphi_{16} :\equiv \forall x\,\forall y\,(x = y \to y = x) \to \forall y\,(q \circ s(n) = y \to y = q \circ s(n))$
\item $\varphi_{17} :\equiv \forall x\,\forall y\,(x = y \to y = x) \to \forall y\,(p \circ s(n) = y \to y = p \circ s(n))$
\item $\varphi_{18} :\equiv \mathrm{SubIdx}(s) \land c \in [a,b] \land \mathrm{Conv}(p \circ s,c) \to c \in \mathbb{R}$
